You are tasked with classifying trees into one of seven classes based on several geographic parameters, including elevation, aspect, slope, distance to hydrology, distance to roadways, and hillshade at various times of the day. Your task is to learn associations between these attributes and the seven possible output classes, and then try your best to assign class labels (y_test) to each of the unlabeled entries in the dataset (X_test).
